7:T7ac,HTTP contract of `apps/graph-api` (dev port 3205, image port 8000;
owner NexGraph). Payload shapes are the JSON Schema components `../schemas/v1/graph-*.schema.json` — not
duplicated here. This file is the ONLY API reference of NexGraph; the portal renders it later.

Every operation runs the PEP pipeline (authenticate → tenancy → OpenFGA → OPA → execute → audit/outbox) and declares
its action in `x-nexus-pep` (`GraphPepAction` vocabulary) `ActorContext` is derived server-side from the bearer or
the service token + propagated human context (grounding calls from chat-api); clients never send authority.
Administering ≠ reading: bindings, grants and runs are readable by administrators without a Project
relation as ids, kinds, states and counts only — titles, knowledge counts and any content require a Project relation.
Hidden and missing objects both return 404 (no existence leak). Denials return coarse `reason_code` +
`user_message_key` (`PepCheckResponse`), never policy text. `verified` / `falsified` are written by exactly one path:
an `OracleRun` (service oracle for deterministic checks, human curator for `human_review`) — no other operation
accepts these statuses. Text leaves graph-api only through `getItemText` / `getPageRender` /
`downloadSourceVersion`, each PEP-checked with classification clearance and `Content-Disposition: attachment`; `POST /graph/resolve` returns refs only. Fail closed: `AUTHZ_MODE=engine` without a valid
signed bundle ⇒ 503 `POLICY_UNAVAILABLE` on every protected operation.

Reason codes: 403 `AUTHOR_NOT_HUMAN` (verify by agent/service) · 403 `PEP_DENIED` · 404 `NOT_FOUND` (hidden or
missing) · 409 `CONNECTION_NEEDS_AUTH` · 409 `GRANT_EXPIRED` · 409 `RUN_NOT_RETRYABLE` · 413 `ITEM_TOO_LARGE` ·
415 `MIME_NOT_ALLOWED` · 422 `POLICY_WIDENS` · 422 `VALIDATION` · 503 `POLICY_UNAVAILABLE` · 503
`CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE` (a model role without a bound model — never a cloud fallback).8:T8c1,1.33.0 (922/925): what a Project binds as a knowledge source. connection_ref is a Synapse connection URI — NexGraph stores no credential; adding a binding never widens the source's ACL. Five independent states, each with a next action. platform_docs bindings are tier=base and read_only. 1.41.0 (1): `revoked` and `sync` (the last sync of a continuous binding — instants, counts, a status; never paths or content); server_folder bindings carry `scope_rules.roots[0] = "<root_id>/<subpath>"` inside the operator's allow-list and `connection_ref: null` (`states.connection = not_applicable`). 1.42.0 (2): google_drive bindings carry `scope_rules.roots[0] = "<drive folder id>"` and `connection_ref = nexus://integration/connection/<id>` (the owner's Synapse connection, `drive.readonly`); `sync.acl_changed` counts the items whose permission list differed at the last sync and `states.acl` follows it (`checking` → `current` | `changed`). 1.60.0: `origin` (SourceOrigin, server-derived) on every binding; `integration_ref` for `kind=connection` (S9-2); `kind` gains `connection` and `web`, `google_drive`/`gmail` are deprecated in place (S9-2 backfills them to `connection`, then refuses new ones with `KIND_DEPRECATED`). 1.66.0 ( + A1.3,, -3): `schedule` on every continuous binding (cadence · cron · timezone · next_run_at · paused; `manual` for `once`); web bindings carry the WebScope fields in `scope_rules` (`start_urls` · `allow_hosts` confirmed by the person · `follow_links` · `max_depth` · `max_pages_per_run` · `goal` · `render` · `respect_robots`), `sync.checkpoint` holds per-URL hints only (never text) and `states.needs_credentials` names the wall the curator met. 1.70.0 (3): `sync.checking_since` and `sync.flow_ref` are set while the platform Flow (`flw_tpl_web_source`) runs for a web binding — the worker acknowledges the hand-over (`sync-dispatched`), the report clears it; `request_sync` is a no-op meanwhile. 1.71.0 (2): `kind=connection` requires `integration_ref` (`nexus://integration/<id>`); `scope_rules` of a connection binding validate against the integration's `knowledge_source.scope_schema` (graph-api, SCOPE_INVALID); `sync.checkpoint.legacy_kind` keeps the backfilled kind for one release.f:X
